## Implementation Plan: GitHub Trajectory Mining Dataset Pipeline

### 1. Design Validation and Issues Found

After reading every relevant file in detail, here is the assessment of the proposed design against the actual codebase.

#### 1a. Compatibility Constraint: The `{task_id, activation_text, teacher_text}` Contract

The existing downstream functions -- `augment_trajectories()`, `split_by_task_id()`, `save_jsonl()`, `load_jsonl()` -- all operate on records with exactly `{task_id, activation_text, teacher_text}`. The `split_by_task_id` function at line 645 of `d2l_data.py` keys on `r["task_id"]`, and `augment_trajectories` at line 598 inherits `task_id`. The proposed `normalize_mined_pairs` output includes a `metadata` field. This is safe -- `save_jsonl` writes arbitrary dicts via `json.dumps`, `load_jsonl` reads them back, and `split_by_task_id` only reads `task_id`. Extra keys pass through harmlessly. **No issue here.**

#### 1b. Existing `normalize_mined_trajectory` vs. New `normalize_mined_pairs`

The existing `normalize_mined_trajectory` (line 341 of `d2l_data.py`) produces ONE record per trajectory, formatted for the old `format_for_distillation` pipeline (which expects `outcome="success"`, `tests_passed=True`, `canonical_solution`). The new `normalize_mined_pairs` produces MULTIPLE records per trajectory (one per step), formatted directly as `{task_id, activation_text, teacher_text}` -- bypassing both `normalize_mined_trajectory` and `format_for_distillation`. This is the correct design. The two paths serve different purposes and should coexist.

#### 1c. Issue: `search_quality_prs` Only Finds Merged PRs

The existing `search_quality_prs` (line 37 of `d2l_mining.py`) builds a query with `is:merged review:approved`. For the DPO negatives pipeline (closed-unmerged PRs), this function cannot be reused directly. The `mine_pr_diff_chains` function (line 120), however, fetches `state: "closed"` and correctly marks `outcome` as `"merged"` or `"closed"` based on `merged_at`. So without `--quality`, the normal mining path already captures both merged and closed-unmerged PRs. **Recommendation: Do NOT add a `--dpo` flag. Instead, mine both in a single pass (the default `mine_pr_diff_chains` already does this), and let `normalize_mined_pairs` tag them via metadata `{outcome: "merged"|"closed"}`. The batch config can control quality filtering per repo.**

#### 1d. Issue: Diff Format Parsing Is Straightforward But Has an Edge Case

The mining output at line 194 of `d2l_mining.py` formats diffs as:
```
--- src/main.py ---\n+real code\n--- package-lock.json ---\n+lockfile noise
```
The separator is `--- {filename} ---`. This is a custom format (not standard unified diff). Parsing requires splitting on lines matching `^--- .+ ---$`. The edge case: if a patch itself contains a line like `--- foo.py ---` (unlikely but possible in test fixtures or documentation). Using `re.compile(r"^--- (.+) ---$", re.MULTILINE)` to split is correct because the mining code only ever produces this exact pattern at section boundaries.

However, there is a **real edge case**: the `mine_issue_commit_chains` function (line 309) uses the identical format. The diff compressor should work for both PR and issue trajectories.

#### 1e. Issue: `content` Can Be Empty

At line 201-203 of `d2l_mining.py`, patches are only appended when `patch` is truthy. If a commit modifies only binary files (no text patches), `content` will be an empty string. The compressor must handle empty input gracefully (return empty string).

### 2. Component Design

#### Component 1: `compress_diff` -- New Module `d2l_diff.py`

**Location:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_diff.py`

**Rationale for separate module:** The diff compression logic is self-contained, testable in isolation, and has no dependencies beyond stdlib. Putting it in `d2l_data.py` would bloat an already 653-line file. A new module `d2l_diff.py` follows the existing `d2l_*` naming convention (`d2l_config.py`, `d2l_data.py`, `d2l_lora.py`, `d2l_mining.py`, `d2l_prep.py`, `d2l_probe.py`, `d2l_train.py`).

**Function signature:**
```python
def compress_diff(content: str, max_lines: int = 500) -> str:
```

**Filter lists (as module-level frozensets):**
- `_SKIP_FILENAMES`: Exact-match lockfiles -- `package-lock.json`, `yarn.lock`, `pnpm-lock.yaml`, `Cargo.lock`, `go.sum`, `Gemfile.lock`, `poetry.lock`, `Pipfile.lock`, `composer.lock`, `flake.lock`
- `_SKIP_EXTENSIONS`: Binary/media -- `.png`, `.jpg`, `.jpeg`, `.gif`, `.svg`, `.ico`, `.woff`, `.woff2`, `.ttf`, `.eot`, `.mp3`, `.mp4`, `.zip`, `.tar`, `.gz`, `.pdf`
- `_SKIP_SUFFIXES`: Generated code -- `_pb2.py`, `_pb2_grpc.py`, `.generated.ts`, `.generated.js`, `.g.dart`, `.min.js`, `.min.css`, `.map`
- `_SKIP_PATH_PREFIXES`: Build artifacts/IDE -- `dist/`, `build/`, `node_modules/`, `.next/`, `__pycache__/`, `.idea/`, `.vscode/`, `.vs/`, `vendor/`

**Algorithm:**
1. If `content` is empty or has no `--- filename ---` sections, fall through to truncation-only (handles issue-commit chains where content might lack section headers).
2. Parse sections using `re.split(r"^(--- .+ ---)$", content, flags=re.MULTILINE)`. This produces alternating [preamble, separator, body, separator, body, ...].
3. For each section, extract filename from the separator, check against all filter lists.
4. Reassemble kept sections.
5. Truncate to `max_lines` total. If truncated, append `\n... [truncated, {original_lines - max_lines} lines omitted]`.

**Edge cases to test:**
- Empty string input -> empty string output
- Content with no section headers -> truncation only
- All sections filtered -> empty string
- Single section that passes -> kept unchanged
- Mixed sections -> only passing ones kept
- Exact `max_lines` -> no truncation marker
- Over `max_lines` -> truncation with marker

#### Component 2: `normalize_mined_pairs` -- New Function in `d2l_data.py`

**Location:** Added to `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_data.py`

**Function signature:**
```python
def normalize_mined_pairs(
    trajectory: dict[str, Any],
    compress: bool = True,
    max_diff_lines: int = 500,
    language: str | None = None,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
```

**Algorithm -- Step Grouping:**

The raw trajectory `steps` are a chronologically interleaved sequence of commits and reviews: `[C1, C2, R1, C3, R2, R3, C4]`. The algorithm must group these into (review_feedback, revision_diff) pairs.

1. Walk through steps, accumulating into "groups" separated by transitions from commit to review or review to commit.
2. Build a list of `(commits_block, reviews_block)` pairs where each block is a contiguous run.
3. For the very first commits block (before any review), the last commit's diff becomes step_0 (initial implementation).
4. For subsequent pairs: the reviews_block text is concatenated as feedback, and the next commits_block's last commit is the revision.

**Detailed step-pair extraction logic:**

```
Given steps: C1, C2, R1, R2, C3, C4, R3, C5

Phase 1 - Group contiguous runs:
  commits_0: [C1, C2]
  reviews_0: [R1, R2]
  commits_1: [C3, C4]
  reviews_1: [R3]
  commits_2: [C5]

Phase 2 - Build pairs:
  Step 0: activation = task_desc, teacher += C2_diff (last of commits_0)
  Step 1: activation = task_desc + C2_diff + "R1\nR2", teacher += C4_diff (last of commits_1)
  Step 2: activation = task_desc + C4_diff + "R3", teacher += C5_diff (last of commits_2)

Trailing reviews (reviews after last commit) are skipped.
```

**Why use last commit of each block:** Multiple commits before a review represent iterative work without feedback. The last one is the state the reviewer actually saw. This preserves the "current state -> feedback -> revision" triplet structure.

**Record format for each step:**
```python
{
    "task_id": f"{trajectory['task_id']}_step_{step_index}",
    "activation_text": activation_text,
    "teacher_text": teacher_text,
    "metadata": {
        "outcome": trajectory["outcome"],  # "merged" or "closed"
        "step_index": step_index,
        "total_steps": total_steps,
        "language": language,  # from repos config, or None
        "source_task_id": trajectory["task_id"],  # for DPO pairing
    },
}
```

**Template for activation/teacher text:**

Step 0 (initial):
```
## Task
{pr_description}
```
teacher appends:
```
\n\n## Implementation\n{compressed_C_diff}
```

Step N (revision):
```
## Task
{pr_description}

## Current Code
{compressed_prev_diff}

## Review Feedback
{concatenated_review_text}
```
teacher appends:
```
\n\n## Revision\n{compressed_C_diff}
```

**Diff compression integration:** Call `compress_diff()` on each commit's `content` field. Import from `d2l_diff`.

**Edge cases to handle (with specific tests):**
- Trajectory with only commits, no reviews -> only step_0 (initial implementation)
- Trajectory with one commit -> one step_0
- Trajectory with commits followed by review followed by no more commits -> step_0 only (trailing review skipped)
- Empty steps list -> empty output
- Commit with empty content (binary-only) -> still produces a pair with empty diff
- Consecutive reviews -> concatenated with newlines
- Multiple commits before first review -> use last commit as step_0 diff

**Critical design decision -- "Current Code" is NOT cumulative:**
The spec says "Current Code" for step N is the previous step's diff, not all prior diffs concatenated. This is correct for the training objective: the model learns "given this diff state and this feedback, produce this revision." Cumulative diffs would blow up context length and dilute the signal.

#### Component 3: Batch Mining CLI Extension

**Location:** Modified in `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/scripts/mine_github.py`

**New arguments:**
- `--batch PATH` (mutually exclusive with `--repo`): Path to JSON config file
- `--output-dir PATH` (required when `--batch`): Directory for per-repo JSONL files
- `--compress/--no-compress` (default `--compress`): Enable/disable diff compression
- `--max-diff-lines N` (default 500): Maximum lines per compressed diff

**Config file format:**
```json
{
    "defaults": {
        "max_prs": 50,
        "quality": true,
        "min_review_comments": 1,
        "min_commits": 2,
        "mode": "prs"
    },
    "repos": [
        {"repo": "huggingface/transformers", "language": "python"},
        {"repo": "astral-sh/ruff", "language": "rust", "max_prs": 100}
    ]
}
```

Per-repo overrides merge with defaults. Only `repo` is required per entry.

**Batch execution flow:**
1. Parse JSON config with `json.load()` (stdlib only).
2. For each repo entry, merge with defaults.
3. Call existing `search_quality_prs()` if `quality` is true.
4. Call existing `mine_pr_diff_chains()`.
5. Call new `normalize_mined_pairs()` on each trajectory.
6. Call existing `save_jsonl()` to `{output_dir}/{owner}_{repo}.jsonl`.
7. Log per-repo and total statistics.

**Backward compatibility:** The `--repo` path remains completely unchanged. The `--batch` path is a new code branch. Use `argparse` mutually exclusive groups.

**Important:** The `--repo` argument must change from `required=True` to required only when `--batch` is not provided. The cleanest approach: make both optional, validate in `main()` that exactly one is provided.

#### Component 4: S3 Upload

No code. Document in the CLI `--help` epilog and a comment:
```
# Upload: aws s3 sync output_dir/ s3://rune-training-data/github-pairs/
```

### 3. Implementation Sequence and File Changes

**Phase A -- Diff Compressor (new file + tests):**

1. Create test file: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_diff.py`
   - 8-10 test functions covering all edge cases listed above
   - Uses no mocking, pure function tests

2. Create module: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_diff.py`
   - `compress_diff(content: str, max_lines: int = 500) -> str`
   - Module-level filter frozensets
   - Helper `_should_skip(filename: str) -> bool`

**Phase B -- Per-Step Pair Extraction (add to existing file + tests):**

3. Add tests to: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_data.py`
   - 8-10 test functions for `normalize_mined_pairs`
   - Reuse existing `_make_pr()` style test helpers (define new ones in the same file)
   - Test edge cases: no reviews, trailing reviews, consecutive commits, DPO metadata, compression integration

4. Add to: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_data.py`
   - `normalize_mined_pairs()` function
   - Add to `__all__`
   - Import `compress_diff` from `d2l_diff`

**Phase C -- Batch CLI (modify existing file + tests):**

5. Add tests to a new file: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/libs/model-training/tests/test_mine_github_batch.py`
   - Test config loading and validation
   - Test batch loop with mocked mining functions
   - Test `--help` for new arguments
   - Keep lightweight -- mock all GitHub calls

6. Modify: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/rune/scripts/mine_github.py`
   - Add `--batch`, `--output-dir`, `--compress`, `--max-diff-lines` arguments
   - Add `_run_batch()` function
   - Make `--repo` not required (validate in `main()`)
   - Import `normalize_mined_pairs` and `save_jsonl` from `d2l_data`, `compress_diff` from `d2l_diff`

### 4. Potential Issues and Mitigations

**Issue 1: Task ID collision across repos in batch mode.**
The existing `task_id` format is `pr_{repo}_{number}`. Since repo names include the owner (`huggingface/transformers`), IDs are globally unique. Step IDs become `pr_huggingface/transformers_42_step_0`. The `/` in the repo name could cause filesystem issues if used in filenames, but task IDs are only used as string keys in JSONL. For output filenames, the batch CLI should sanitize: `huggingface_transformers.jsonl` (replace `/` with `_`).

**Issue 2: Rate limiting across batch repos.**
The existing `GitHubClient` handles rate-limit retries per request. For batch mining of 10+ repos, the cumulative API calls could hit the 5000/hour limit. Mitigation: log the remaining rate limit after each repo (available in `X-RateLimit-Remaining` response header). This is an operational concern, not a code design issue. The batch CLI should log a summary at the end.

**Issue 3: `compress_diff` dependency from `d2l_data.py`.**
Importing `d2l_diff` from `d2l_data` creates a new intra-package dependency. Since `d2l_diff` is stdlib-only (re, no GPU imports), this is safe and consistent with the INFRA-05 deferred-import pattern noted in CLAUDE.md. The import should be at the top of `d2l_data.py` since `d2l_diff` has no heavy dependencies.

**Issue 4: DPO negative examples need careful handling downstream.**
The `normalize_mined_pairs` function will produce records for both merged (positive) and closed-unmerged (negative) PRs. The `metadata.outcome` field distinguishes them. Downstream, the QLoRA fine-tuning script will need to filter by `metadata.outcome == "merged"` for standard SFT, and pair merged/closed trajectories for DPO. This is a training-time concern, not a data-pipeline concern. The pipeline should produce both and let the training script filter.

**Issue 5: `search_quality_prs` only finds merged PRs for quality filtering.**
When batch mode uses `quality: true`, only merged PRs are pre-filtered. To also get quality closed-unmerged PRs, we would need a second search query. However, closed-unmerged PRs inherently lack `review:approved`, making quality pre-filtering less useful for them. **Recommendation:** For the batch pipeline, run `search_quality_prs` for merged (positive) PRs, and separately fetch a smaller sample of closed-unmerged PRs without quality filtering. Add an optional `"include_closed": true` config field that triggers a second `mine_pr_diff_chains` call without `pr_numbers` filtering but limited to `max_prs / 5` closed-unmerged PRs.

Actually, re-reading the mining code more carefully: `mine_pr_diff_chains` without `pr_numbers` fetches ALL closed PRs (both merged and unmerged) via `state: "closed"`. So when quality filtering is off, both types are already captured. When quality filtering is on, only merged are captured. The simplest design: in batch mode, always run two passes -- one with quality filtering for merged PRs, one without for a small sample of closed-unmerged. This can be a follow-up enhancement. For the initial implementation, just mining with quality on (merged only) is sufficient for Stage 1 QLoRA.

### 5. Answers to the Design Questions

**Q1: Should `compress_diff` handle content with no "--- filename ---" sections?**
Yes. The function checks for section headers first. If none are found, it falls through to truncation-only. This handles issue-commit chains where the mining may produce different content formats, and also handles edge cases where a commit has only one file (the content would still have a header in the current mining code, but defensive coding is correct).

**Q2: How should `normalize_mined_pairs` handle consecutive commits without reviews?**
Group contiguous commits into a block. Use the last commit in the block as the representative diff. For the first block (before any review), this becomes step_0's implementation diff. For later blocks, this becomes the revision diff following the preceding review block.

**Q3: Should metadata include language from the repos config?**
Yes. Pass it through `normalize_mined_pairs(trajectory, language="python")`. The batch CLI reads `language` from the repo config and forwards it. For single-repo mode, language is `None`. The field is purely informational -- used for analysis and dataset stratification, not for training logic.

**Q4: Should we add a `--dpo` flag?**
No. Mine both in a single pass. The existing `mine_pr_diff_chains` with `state: "closed"` already captures both merged and closed-unmerged PRs. Tag via `metadata.outcome`. Let the training script handle DPO pairing. Adding a flag creates unnecessary complexity for no benefit -- the metadata-based approach is more flexible.

### 6. Test Coverage Strategy

All tests use the existing patterns: `pytest` with `tmp_path`, `@patch("model_training.d2l_mining.GitHubClient")` for API mocking, and inline helper factories (not conftest fixtures, matching the `test_d2l_data.py` and `test_d2l_mining.py` patterns).

**test_d2l_diff.py (8 tests):**
- `test_compress_diff_empty_input`
- `test_compress_diff_no_sections_truncates`
- `test_compress_diff_keeps_source_files`
- `test_compress_diff_filters_lockfiles`
- `test_compress_diff_filters_generated_code`
- `test_compress_diff_filters_binary_extensions`
- `test_compress_diff_filters_build_paths`
- `test_compress_diff_truncates_at_max_lines`
- `test_compress_diff_all_filtered_returns_empty`

**test_d2l_data.py additions (8 tests):**
- `test_normalize_mined_pairs_basic_commit_review_commit`
- `test_normalize_mined_pairs_step0_has_task_and_implementation`
- `test_normalize_mined_pairs_step1_has_current_code_and_feedback`

- `test_normalize_mined_pairs_consecutive_reviews_concatenated`
- `test_normalize_mined_pairs_trailing_reviews_skipped`

- `test_normalize_mined_pairs_metadata_includes_outcome_and_language`
- `test_normalize_mined_pairs_records_compatible_with_split_by_task_id`

1	"""GitHub trajectory mining CLI.
2	
3	Mines GitHub repositories into trajectory JSON files suitable for
4	Doc-to-LoRA distillation. Extracts PR diff chains and/or issue-commit
5	chains and optionally normalizes them for the distillation pipeline.
6	
7	Usage:
8	    uv run python scripts/mine_github.py --repo owner/repo -o out.json
9	    uv run python scripts/mine_github.py --repo owner/repo --mode prs --max 50 -o prs.json
10	    uv run python scripts/mine_github.py --repo owner/repo --mode issues --normalize -o issues.json
11	    uv run python scripts/mine_github.py --repo owner/repo --token ghp_xxx -o both.json
12	"""
13

15

17	import json
18	import logging
19	import os
20	import sys
21	from pathlib import Path

25	    format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s: %(message)s",
26	)
27	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
28	
29	
30	def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
31	    """Parse command-line arguments."""
32	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
33	        description="Mine a GitHub repository into trajectory JSON files.",
34	        formatter_class=argparse.ArgumentDefaultsHelpFormatter,
35	    )
36	    parser.add_argument(
37	        "--repo",
38	        required=True,
39	        metavar="OWNER/REPO",
40	        help='GitHub repository in "owner/repo" format.',
41	    )
42	    parser.add_argument(
43	        "--mode",
44	        choices=["prs", "issues", "both"],
45	        default="both",
46	        help="Mining mode: pull requests, issues, or both.",
47	    )
48	    parser.add_argument(
49	        "--max",
50	        type=int,
51	        default=100,
52	        dest="max_items",
53	        metavar="N",
54	        help="Maximum PRs/issues to process.",
55	    )
56	    parser.add_argument(
57	        "--token",
58	        default=None,
59	        metavar="TOKEN",
60	        help="GitHub personal access token (falls back to GITHUB_TOKEN env var).",
61	    )
62	    parser.add_argument(
63	        "-o",
64	        "--output",
65	        required=True,
66	        type=Path,
67	        metavar="FILE",
68	        help="Output JSON file path.",
69	    )
70	    parser.add_argument(
71	        "--normalize",
72	        action="store_true",
73	        help="Normalize trajectories for distillation compatibility.",
74	    )
75	    parser.add_argument(
76	        "--quality",
77	        action="store_true",
78	        help="Pre-filter PRs via GitHub Search API for quality (merged, reviewed, multi-commit).",
79	    )
80	    parser.add_argument(
81	        "--min-reviews",
82	        type=int,
83	        default=1,
84	        metavar="N",
85	        help="Minimum review comments for quality filter.",
86	    )
87	    parser.add_argument(
88	        "--min-commits",
89	        type=int,
90	        default=2,
91	        metavar="N",
92	        help="Minimum commits for quality filter.",
93	    )
94	    parser.add_argument(
95	        "--exclude-labels",
96	        default=None,
97	        metavar="L1,L2,...",
98	        help="Comma-separated labels to exclude (default: dependencies,documentation,docs,chore,ci,bot).",
99	    )
100	    return parser.parse_args()
101	
102	
103	def main() -> None:
104	    """Entry point for the mine_github CLI."""
105	    args = parse_args()
106	
107	    # Resolve GitHub token
108	    token: str | None = args.token or os.environ.get("GITHUB_TOKEN")
109	    if not token:
110	        logger.error(
111	            "No GitHub token provided. Use --token or set the GITHUB_TOKEN env var."
112	        )
113	        sys.exit(1)
114	
115	    from model_training.d2l_data import normalize_mined_trajectory
116	    from model_training.d2l_mining import (
117	        mine_issue_commit_chains,
118	        mine_pr_diff_chains,

125

145	                exclude_labels=exclude_labels,
146	            )
147	            logger.info("Quality filter selected %d PR(s).", len(pr_numbers))
148	
149	        logger.info(

151	        )

155	            github_token=token,
156	            pr_numbers=pr_numbers,
157	        )
158	        logger.info("Mined %d PR diff chain(s).", len(pr_trajectories))
159	        trajectories.extend(pr_trajectories)
160	
161	    # Mine issue-commit chains
162	    if args.mode in ("issues", "both"):
163	        logger.info(
164	            "Mining issue-commit chains from %s (max=%d)...", args.repo, args.max_items
165	        )
166	        issue_trajectories = mine_issue_commit_chains(
167	            args.repo,
168	            max_issues=args.max_items,
169	            github_token=token,
170	        )
171	        logger.info("Mined %d issue-commit chain(s).", len(issue_trajectories))
172	        trajectories.extend(issue_trajectories)
173	
174	    logger.info("Total trajectories collected: %d.", len(trajectories))
175	
176	    # Normalize if requested
177	    if args.normalize:
178	        logger.info("Normalizing %d trajectory/trajectories...", len(trajectories))
179	        trajectories = [normalize_mined_trajectory(t) for t in trajectories]
180	        logger.info("Normalization complete.")
181	
182	    # Write output
183	    output: Path = args.output
184	    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
185	    with output.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
186	        json.dump(trajectories, fh, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
187	    logger.info("Wrote %d trajectory/trajectories to %s.", len(trajectories), output)
188	
189	
190	if __name__ == "__main__":
191	    main()

Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.\n\n**Goal:** Build the dataset preparation pipeline that mines GitHub PRs into per-step training pairs with compressed diffs, outputting distillation-ready JSONL for S3 upload to train Rune's hypernetwork.\n\n**Architecture:** Three new components extend the existing mining pipeline: (1) `d2l_diff` module for RTK-style diff filtering, (2) `normalize_mined_pairs()` function producing one training record per review-to-revision cycle, (3) batch mining CLI processing a repos config. Output JSONL is compatible with existing `augment_trajectories()`, `split_by_task_id()`, and `save_jsonl()`. Mining runs on CPU; prepared JSONL uploads to S3 for L4 GPU training.\n\n**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12, httpx, json (stdlib), pytest\n\n---\n\n## Context\n\nRune's hypernetwork (Sakana Doc-to-LoRA perceiver) generates step-level LoRA adapters encoding procedural coding knowledge in weight space. The thesis: weight-space episodic memory outperforms context-window approaches for steering small models through complex multi-step coding projects.\n\nThis pipeline mines GitHub PRs from curated repositories, extracts per-step training pairs (review feedback -> code revision), compresses diffs to remove noise, and outputs distillation-ready JSONL. The data feeds Stage 1 (QLoRA fine-tuning with DeltaCoder warm-start on Qwen3.5-9B) which produces the adapter corpus for Stage 2 (hypernetwork training).\n\nEach training pair represents one step:\n- **activation_text** (hypernetwork input): PR description + current code state + review feedback\n- **teacher_text** (ground truth): activation_text + the correct revision diff\n\nKey design decisions from user interview:\n- Each adapter encodes ONE step, not an entire trajectory\n- \"Current Code\" = previous step's diff only (not cumulative)\n- Multi-file commits = one training pair (preserves cross-file coherence)\n- Merged PRs = positive examples; closed-unmerged = DPO negatives (tagged via metadata)\n- Diff compression filters lockfiles, generated code, binary files (RTK-style)\n- Minimum changes to existing code; reuse existing pipeline functions\n\n---\n\n## File Structure\n\n**Create:**\n- `libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_diff.py` -- diff compression (follows `d2l_*` naming convention)\n- `libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_diff.py` -- compressor tests\n- `instructions/mining_repos.json` -- Phase 1 repos config (10 repos)\n\n**Modify:**\n- `libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_data.py:20-29,653` -- add `normalize_mined_pairs()` + update `__all__`\n- `libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_data.py` -- add pair extraction tests\n- `scripts/mine_github.py:30-101,103-191` -- add batch mining mode\n\n**Existing code reused unchanged:**\n- `d2l_mining.py`: `mine_pr_diff_chains()`, `search_quality_prs()` -- mining core\n- `d2l_data.py`: `augment_trajectories()`, `split_by_task_id()`, `save_jsonl()`, `load_jsonl()` -- downstream pipeline\n- `github_client.py`: `GitHubClient` -- HTTP client with rate-limit retry\n\n---\n\n## Repo Selection Strategy\n\n**Phase 1 (Bootstrap): 10 repos, ~500 PRs, ~1500 step-level pairs**\n\n| Language | Repos | Rationale |\n|---|---|---|\n| Python (4) | huggingface/transformers, scikit-learn/scikit-learn, fastapi/fastapi, apache/airflow | Rich review culture, diverse domains (ML, algorithms, web, orchestration) |\n| TypeScript (2) | microsoft/TypeScript, vercel/next.js | Exceptional type-system reviews, high-velocity web |\n| Rust (2) | denoland/deno, rust-lang/rust-analyzer | Systems + IDE compiler, clean MIT |\n| Go (2) | pingcap/tidb, kubernetes/kubernetes | Bot-enforced issue linkage, richest review culture |\n\n50 quality-filtered PRs per repo via `search_quality_prs()`.\n\n**Phase 2 (Expand, after Stage 1 validation):** +15 repos adding C++ (llvm, duckdb, godot), C# (roslyn, runtime), Kotlin (okhttp), Dart (flutter), Scala (scala3), Solidity (OpenZeppelin), plus more Python/Rust/Go.\n\n**Phase 3 (Conditional):** Niche repos only if evaluation shows domain gaps.\n\n---\n\n### Task 1: Diff Compressor Module\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_diff.py`\n- Create: `libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_diff.py`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Write failing tests for diff compression**\n\n```python\n# libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_diff.py\n\"\"\"Tests for model_training.d2l_diff module.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\n\ndef test_compress_diff_empty_input() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Empty string passes through unchanged.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_diff import compress_diff\n\n    assert compress_diff(\"\") == \"\"\n\n\ndef test_compress_diff_no_sections_truncates() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Content without section headers falls through to truncation.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_diff import compress_diff\n\n    content = \"\\n\".join(f\"+line {i}\" for i in range(100))\n    result = compress_diff(content, max_lines=10)\n    lines = result.split(\"\\n\")\n    assert len(lines) == 11  # 10 kept + truncation message\n    assert \"truncated\" in lines[-1]\n\n\ndef test_compress_diff_keeps_source_files() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Source code files are kept.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_diff import compress_diff\n\n    content = \"--- src/main.py ---\\n+def hello(): pass\"\n    result = compress_diff(content)\n    assert \"src/main.py\" in result\n    assert \"+def hello(): pass\" in result\n\n\ndef test_compress_diff_filters_lockfiles() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Lockfiles are removed.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_diff import compress_diff\n\n    content = (\n        \"--- src/main.py ---\\n+real code\\n\"\n        \"--- package-lock.json ---\\n+lock noise\"\n    )\n    result = compress_diff(content)\n    assert \"src/main.py\" in result\n    assert \"package-lock.json\" not in result\n    assert \"lock noise\" not in result\n\n\ndef test_compress_diff_filters_generated_code() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Generated protobuf files are removed.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_diff import compress_diff\n\n    content = (\n        \"--- src/handler.go ---\\n+real code\\n\"\n        \"--- proto/service_pb2.py ---\\n+generated\"\n    )\n    result = compress_diff(content)\n    assert \"handler.go\" in result\n    assert \"service_pb2.py\" not in result\n\n\ndef test_compress_diff_filters_binary_extensions() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Binary and media files are removed.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_diff import compress_diff\n\n    content = (\n        \"--- src/app.ts ---\\n+code\\n\"\n        \"--- assets/logo.png ---\\n+binary\"\n    )\n    result = compress_diff(content)\n    assert \"app.ts\" in result\n    assert \"logo.png\" not in result\n\n\ndef test_compress_diff_filters_build_paths() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Build artifact paths are removed.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_diff import compress_diff\n\n    content = (\n        \"--- src/lib.rs ---\\n+code\\n\"\n        \"--- dist/bundle.js ---\\n+built\\n\"\n        \"--- node_modules/foo/index.js ---\\n+dep\"\n    )\n    result = compress_diff(content)\n    assert \"lib.rs\" in result\n    assert \"dist/\" not in result\n    assert \"node_modules/\" not in result\n\n\ndef test_compress_diff_truncates_at_max_lines() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Output respects max_lines limit.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_diff import compress_diff\n\n    lines = [f\"+line {i}\" for i in range(200)]\n    content = \"--- big.py ---\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(lines)\n    result = compress_diff(content, max_lines=50)\n    result_lines = result.split(\"\\n\")\n    assert len(result_lines) <= 51  # 50 + truncation message\n    assert \"truncated\" in result_lines[-1]\n\n\ndef test_compress_diff_all_filtered_returns_empty() -> None:\n    \"\"\"When all sections are filtered, return empty string.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_diff import compress_diff\n\n    content = (\n        \"--- package-lock.json ---\\n+lock\\n\"\n        \"--- yarn.lock ---\\n+yarn\\n\"\n        \"--- dist/out.js ---\\n+built\"\n    )\n    result = compress_diff(content)\n    assert result == \"\"\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run tests to verify they fail**\n\nRun: `uv run pytest libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_diff.py -v`\nExpected: FAIL (ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'model_training.d2l_diff')\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Implement d2l_diff.py**\n\n```python\n# libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_diff.py\n\"\"\"RTK-style diff compression for training data preparation.\n\nFilters irrelevant files (lockfiles, generated code, binary assets,\nbuild artifacts) and truncates large diffs to minimize token overhead\nin hypernetwork training pairs.\n\nOperates on the concatenated diff format produced by mine_pr_diff_chains:\n    --- src/main.py ---\n    +real code\n    --- package-lock.json ---\n    +lockfile noise\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nfrom pathlib import PurePosixPath\n\n__all__ = [\"compress_diff\"]\n\n_SKIP_FILENAMES: frozenset[str] = frozenset(\n    {\n        \"package-lock.json\",\n        \"yarn.lock\",\n        \"pnpm-lock.yaml\",\n        \"poetry.lock\",\n        \"uv.lock\",\n        \"Pipfile.lock\",\n        \"Cargo.lock\",\n        \"go.sum\",\n        \"Gemfile.lock\",\n        \"composer.lock\",\n        \"flake.lock\",\n        \".DS_Store\",\n        \".gitattributes\",\n        \".editorconfig\",\n    }\n)\n\n_SKIP_EXTENSIONS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(\n    {\n        \".lock\",\n        \".png\",\n        \".jpg\",\n        \".jpeg\",\n        \".gif\",\n        \".svg\",\n        \".ico\",\n        \".woff\",\n        \".woff2\",\n        \".ttf\",\n        \".eot\",\n        \".mp3\",\n        \".mp4\",\n        \".zip\",\n        \".tar\",\n        \".gz\",\n        \".pdf\",\n        \".pyc\",\n        \".pyo\",\n        \".so\",\n        \".dylib\",\n        \".dll\",\n    }\n)\n\n_SKIP_SUFFIXES: tuple[str, ...] = (\n    \"_pb2.py\",\n    \"_pb2_grpc.py\",\n    \".pb.go\",\n    \".pb.h\",\n    \".pb.cc\",\n    \".generated.ts\",\n    \".generated.js\",\n    \".g.dart\",\n    \".min.js\",\n    \".min.css\",\n    \".map\",\n)\n\n_SKIP_PATH_SEGMENTS: tuple[str, ...] = (\n    \"node_modules/\",\n    \"__pycache__/\",\n    \".venv/\",\n    \"vendor/\",\n    \"dist/\",\n    \"build/\",\n    \".next/\",\n    \"coverage/\",\n    \".idea/\",\n    \".vscode/\",\n)\n\n_SECTION_RE = re.compile(r\"^(--- .+ ---)$\", re.MULTILINE)\n\n\ndef _should_skip(filename: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Check if a file should be filtered from training data.\"\"\"\n    path = PurePosixPath(filename)\n    if path.name in _SKIP_FILENAMES:\n        return True\n    if path.suffix in _SKIP_EXTENSIONS:\n        return True\n    if any(filename.endswith(s) for s in _SKIP_SUFFIXES):\n        return True\n    return any(seg in filename for seg in _SKIP_PATH_SEGMENTS)\n\n\ndef _truncate(text: str, max_lines: int) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Truncate text to max_lines, appending a marker if truncated.\"\"\"\n    lines = text.split(\"\\n\")\n    if len(lines) <= max_lines:\n        return text\n    kept = lines[:max_lines]\n    kept.append(f\"... ({len(lines) - max_lines} lines truncated)\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(kept)\n\n\ndef compress_diff(content: str, max_lines: int = 500) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Filter irrelevant files and truncate large diffs.\n\n    Parses the ``--- filename ---`` section format produced by\n    ``mine_pr_diff_chains`` and removes lockfiles, generated code,\n    binary files, and build artifacts.\n\n    Args:\n        content: Concatenated diff content with section headers.\n        max_lines: Maximum total output lines.\n\n    Returns:\n        Filtered and truncated diff string.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not content:\n        return content\n\n    parts = _SECTION_RE.split(content)\n\n    # No section headers found -- fall through to truncation only\n    if len(parts) == 1:\n        return _truncate(content, max_lines)\n\n    # parts = [preamble, header1, body1, header2, body2, ...]\n    # re.split with a capturing group interleaves captured groups\n    kept: list[str] = []\n    idx = 1  # skip preamble (parts[0])\n    while idx < len(parts):\n        header = parts[idx]\n        body = parts[idx + 1] if idx + 1 < len(parts) else \"\"\n        # Extract filename from \"--- filename ---\"\n        filename = header[4:-4]  # strip \"--- \" and \" ---\"\n        if not _should_skip(filename):\n            kept.append(header + body)\n        idx += 2\n\n    result = \"\".join(kept).strip()\n\n    if not result:\n        return \"\"\n\n    return _truncate(result, max_lines)\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run tests to verify they pass**\n\nRun: `uv run pytest libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_diff.py -v`\nExpected: 9 passed\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Run linting**\n\nRun: `uv run ruff check libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_diff.py libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_diff.py`\nExpected: clean\n\n- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_diff.py libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_diff.py\ngit commit -m \"feat: add d2l_diff module for RTK-style diff compression\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 2: Per-Step Training Pair Extraction\n\n**Files:**\n- Modify: `libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_data.py`\n- Modify: `libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_data.py`\n\n- [ ] **Step 1: Write failing tests for normalize_mined_pairs**\n\nAppend to `libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_data.py`:\n\n```python\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Helpers for normalize_mined_pairs tests\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef _make_mined_trajectory(\n    task_id: str = \"pr_owner/repo_42\",\n    task_description: str = \"Add widget support\",\n    steps: list[dict[str, str]] | None = None,\n    outcome: str = \"merged\",\n) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Create a minimal mined trajectory dict for normalize_mined_pairs tests.\"\"\"\n    if steps is None:\n        steps = [\n            {\"type\": \"commit\", \"description\": \"Initial impl\", \"content\": \"+def widget(): pass\"},\n        ]\n    return {\n        \"task_id\": task_id,\n        \"task_description\": task_description,\n        \"steps\": steps,\n        \"outcome\": outcome,\n    }\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Test 11-19: normalize_mined_pairs\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef test_normalize_mined_pairs_single_commit_produces_step0() -> None:\n    \"\"\"A single-commit PR produces one step_0 pair.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_data import normalize_mined_pairs\n\n    trajectory = _make_mined_trajectory()\n    pairs = normalize_mined_pairs(trajectory)\n\n    assert len(pairs) == 1\n    assert pairs[0][\"task_id\"] == \"pr_owner/repo_42_step_0\"\n    assert \"Add widget support\" in pairs[0][\"activation_text\"]\n    assert \"+def widget(): pass\" in pairs[0][\"teacher_text\"]\n    assert \"## Implementation\" in pairs[0][\"teacher_text\"]\n\n\ndef test_normalize_mined_pairs_review_revision_cycle() -> None:\n    \"\"\"A commit-review-commit trajectory produces step_0 + step_1.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_data import normalize_mined_pairs\n\n    trajectory = _make_mined_trajectory(steps=[\n        {\"type\": \"commit\", \"description\": \"V1\", \"content\": \"+v1 code\"},\n        {\"type\": \"review\", \"description\": \"Review\", \"content\": \"Add error handling\"},\n        {\"type\": \"commit\", \"description\": \"V2\", \"content\": \"+v2 with error handling\"},\n    ])\n    pairs = normalize_mined_pairs(trajectory)\n\n    assert len(pairs) == 2\n    # Step 0: initial implementation\n    assert pairs[0][\"task_id\"] == \"pr_owner/repo_42_step_0\"\n    assert \"## Task\" in pairs[0][\"activation_text\"]\n    assert \"## Implementation\" in pairs[0][\"teacher_text\"]\n    # Step 1: review -> revision\n    assert pairs[1][\"task_id\"] == \"pr_owner/repo_42_step_1\"\n    assert \"## Current Code\" in pairs[1][\"activation_text\"]\n    assert \"+v1 code\" in pairs[1][\"activation_text\"]\n    assert \"## Review Feedback\" in pairs[1][\"activation_text\"]\n    assert \"Add error handling\" in pairs[1][\"activation_text\"]\n    assert \"## Revision\" in pairs[1][\"teacher_text\"]\n    assert \"+v2 with error handling\" in pairs[1][\"teacher_text\"]\n\n\ndef test_normalize_mined_pairs_consecutive_commits_uses_last() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Multiple commits before first review: last commit used for step_0.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_data import normalize_mined_pairs\n\n    trajectory = _make_mined_trajectory(steps=[\n        {\"type\": \"commit\", \"description\": \"Draft\", \"content\": \"+draft\"},\n        {\"type\": \"commit\", \"description\": \"Polish\", \"content\": \"+polished\"},\n        {\"type\": \"review\", \"description\": \"Review\", \"content\": \"Fix typo\"},\n        {\"type\": \"commit\", \"description\": \"Fixed\", \"content\": \"+fixed\"},\n    ])\n    pairs = normalize_mined_pairs(trajectory)\n\n    assert len(pairs) == 2\n    # Step 0 uses last commit before review\n    assert \"+polished\" in pairs[0][\"teacher_text\"]\n    assert \"+draft\" not in pairs[0][\"teacher_text\"]\n    # Step 1: previous diff is the polished version\n    assert \"+polished\" in pairs[1][\"activation_text\"]\n\n\ndef test_normalize_mined_pairs_consecutive_reviews_concatenated() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Multiple reviews before a commit are concatenated.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_data import normalize_mined_pairs\n\n    trajectory = _make_mined_trajectory(steps=[\n        {\"type\": \"commit\", \"description\": \"V1\", \"content\": \"+v1\"},\n        {\"type\": \"review\", \"description\": \"R1\", \"content\": \"Fix A\"},\n        {\"type\": \"review\", \"description\": \"R2\", \"content\": \"Also fix B\"},\n        {\"type\": \"commit\", \"description\": \"V2\", \"content\": \"+v2\"},\n    ])\n    pairs = normalize_mined_pairs(trajectory)\n\n    assert len(pairs) == 2\n    assert \"Fix A\" in pairs[1][\"activation_text\"]\n    assert \"Also fix B\" in pairs[1][\"activation_text\"]\n\n\ndef test_normalize_mined_pairs_trailing_reviews_skipped() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Reviews after the last commit are ignored.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_data import normalize_mined_pairs\n\n    trajectory = _make_mined_trajectory(steps=[\n        {\"type\": \"commit\", \"description\": \"V1\", \"content\": \"+v1\"},\n        {\"type\": \"review\", \"description\": \"Review\", \"content\": \"Needs work\"},\n    ])\n    pairs = normalize_mined_pairs(trajectory)\n\n    # Only step_0 from the initial commit; trailing review is skipped\n    assert len(pairs) == 1\n    assert pairs[0][\"task_id\"] == \"pr_owner/repo_42_step_0\"\n\n\ndef test_normalize_mined_pairs_empty_steps_returns_empty() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Empty steps list produces no pairs.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_data import normalize_mined_pairs\n\n    trajectory = _make_mined_trajectory(steps=[])\n    assert normalize_mined_pairs(trajectory) == []\n\n\ndef test_normalize_mined_pairs_metadata_includes_outcome_and_language() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Metadata captures outcome and language from caller.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_data import normalize_mined_pairs\n\n    merged = _make_mined_trajectory(outcome=\"merged\")\n    closed = _make_mined_trajectory(outcome=\"closed\")\n\n    merged_pairs = normalize_mined_pairs(merged, language=\"python\")\n    closed_pairs = normalize_mined_pairs(closed, language=\"go\")\n\n    assert merged_pairs[0][\"metadata\"][\"outcome\"] == \"merged\"\n    assert merged_pairs[0][\"metadata\"][\"language\"] == \"python\"\n    assert closed_pairs[0][\"metadata\"][\"outcome\"] == \"closed\"\n    assert closed_pairs[0][\"metadata\"][\"language\"] == \"go\"\n\n\ndef test_normalize_mined_pairs_compatible_with_split_by_task_id() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Output records work with existing split_by_task_id.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_data import normalize_mined_pairs, split_by_task_id\n\n    t1 = _make_mined_trajectory(task_id=\"pr_a/b_1\", steps=[\n        {\"type\": \"commit\", \"description\": \"C1\", \"content\": \"+c1\"},\n        {\"type\": \"review\", \"description\": \"R1\", \"content\": \"Fix\"},\n        {\"type\": \"commit\", \"description\": \"C2\", \"content\": \"+c2\"},\n    ])\n    t2 = _make_mined_trajectory(task_id=\"pr_c/d_2\", steps=[\n        {\"type\": \"commit\", \"description\": \"C1\", \"content\": \"+c1\"},\n    ])\n\n    all_pairs = normalize_mined_pairs(t1) + normalize_mined_pairs(t2)\n    train, test = split_by_task_id(all_pairs, test_fraction=0.5, seed=42)\n\n    train_ids = {r[\"task_id\"] for r in train}\n    test_ids = {r[\"task_id\"] for r in test}\n    assert not train_ids & test_ids\n\n\ndef test_normalize_mined_pairs_multiple_rounds() -> None:\n    \"\"\"A complex PR with 3 review rounds produces 4 pairs.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_data import normalize_mined_pairs\n\n    trajectory = _make_mined_trajectory(steps=[\n        {\"type\": \"commit\", \"description\": \"V1\", \"content\": \"+v1\"},\n        {\"type\": \"review\", \"description\": \"R1\", \"content\": \"Fix X\"},\n        {\"type\": \"commit\", \"description\": \"V2\", \"content\": \"+v2\"},\n        {\"type\": \"review\", \"description\": \"R2\", \"content\": \"Fix Y\"},\n        {\"type\": \"commit\", \"description\": \"V3\", \"content\": \"+v3\"},\n        {\"type\": \"review\", \"description\": \"R3\", \"content\": \"Fix Z\"},\n        {\"type\": \"commit\", \"description\": \"V4\", \"content\": \"+v4\"},\n    ])\n    pairs = normalize_mined_pairs(trajectory)\n\n    assert len(pairs) == 4  # step_0 + 3 review->revision cycles\n    assert pairs[0][\"task_id\"].endswith(\"_step_0\")\n    assert pairs[1][\"task_id\"].endswith(\"_step_1\")\n    assert pairs[2][\"task_id\"].endswith(\"_step_2\")\n    assert pairs[3][\"task_id\"].endswith(\"_step_3\")\n    # Each step's \"Current Code\" is the PREVIOUS step's diff (not cumulative)\n    assert \"+v2\" in pairs[2][\"activation_text\"]  # step_2 sees v2 as current\n    assert \"+v1\" not in pairs[2][\"activation_text\"]  # not cumulative\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run tests to verify they fail**\n\nRun: `uv run pytest libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_data.py -k \"normalize_mined_pairs\" -v`\nExpected: FAIL (ImportError: cannot import name 'normalize_mined_pairs')\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Implement normalize_mined_pairs in d2l_data.py**\n\nAdd import at top of `d2l_data.py` (after existing imports, line ~16):\n\n```python\nfrom model_training.d2l_diff import compress_diff\n```\n\nAdd `\"normalize_mined_pairs\"` to `__all__` list (line ~20-29).\n\nAppend after `split_by_task_id` (after line 652):\n\n```python\ndef normalize_mined_pairs(\n    trajectory: dict[str, Any],\n    compress: bool = True,\n    max_diff_lines: int = 500,\n    language: str | None = None,\n) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Convert a mined PR trajectory into per-step training pairs.\n\n    Each review-to-revision cycle becomes one training record with\n    activation_text (task + current code + review feedback) and\n    teacher_text (activation + revision diff). Compatible with\n    ``augment_trajectories``, ``split_by_task_id``, and ``save_jsonl``.\n\n    The algorithm groups contiguous commits and reviews into blocks,\n    then pairs each reviews-block with the following commits-block.\n    Multiple commits in a block: the last commit is used (the state\n    the reviewer actually saw). Multiple reviews: concatenated.\n\n    Args:\n        trajectory: Raw mined trajectory from ``mine_pr_diff_chains``.\n        compress: Apply diff compression via ``compress_diff``.\n        max_diff_lines: Max lines per compressed diff.\n        language: Language tag for metadata (from repos config).\n\n    Returns:\n        List of training pair records with task_id, activation_text,\n        teacher_text, and metadata fields.\n    \"\"\"\n    task_id: str = trajectory.get(\"task_id\", \"\")\n    task_desc: str = trajectory.get(\"task_description\", \"\")\n    raw_steps: list[dict[str, Any]] = trajectory.get(\"steps\", [])\n    outcome: str = trajectory.get(\"outcome\", \"\")\n\n    if not raw_steps:\n        return []\n\n    # --- Group contiguous runs of commits / reviews ---\n    blocks: list[tuple[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]] = []\n    cur_type: str | None = None\n    cur_block: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    for step in raw_steps:\n        stype = step.get(\"type\", \"\")\n        if stype != cur_type:\n            if cur_block:\n                blocks.append((cur_type or \"\", cur_block))\n            cur_type = stype\n            cur_block = [step]\n        else:\n            cur_block.append(step)\n    if cur_block:\n        blocks.append((cur_type or \"\", cur_block))\n\n    def _diff(step: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n        raw = step.get(\"content\", \"\")\n        return compress_diff(raw, max_lines=max_diff_lines) if compress else raw\n\n    def _record(idx: int, activation: str, teacher: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        return {\n            \"task_id\": f\"{task_id}_step_{idx}\",\n            \"activation_text\": activation,\n            \"teacher_text\": teacher,\n            \"metadata\": {\n                \"outcome\": outcome,\n                \"step_index\": idx,\n                \"language\": language,\n                \"source_task_id\": task_id,\n            },\n        }\n\n    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    step_idx = 0\n    prev_diff = \"\"\n    bi = 0  # block index\n\n    # --- Step 0: initial commits block ---\n    if blocks[0][0] == \"commit\":\n        last_commit = blocks[0][1][-1]\n        diff = _diff(last_commit)\n        activation = f\"## Task\\n{task_desc}\"\n        teacher = f\"{activation}\\n\\n## Implementation\\n{diff}\"\n        records.append(_record(step_idx, activation, teacher))\n        prev_diff = diff\n        step_idx += 1\n        bi = 1\n\n    # --- Subsequent (reviews, commits) pairs ---\n    while bi < len(blocks) - 1:\n        if blocks[bi][0] == \"review\" and blocks[bi + 1][0] == \"commit\":\n            review_text = \"\\n\\n\".join(\n                r.get(\"content\", \"\") for r in blocks[bi][1]\n            )\n            last_commit = blocks[bi + 1][1][-1]\n            revision = _diff(last_commit)\n\n            activation = f\"## Task\\n{task_desc}\"\n            if prev_diff:\n                activation += f\"\\n\\n## Current Code\\n{prev_diff}\"\n            activation += f\"\\n\\n## Review Feedback\\n{review_text}\"\n            teacher = f\"{activation}\\n\\n## Revision\\n{revision}\"\n\n            records.append(_record(step_idx, activation, teacher))\n            prev_diff = revision\n            step_idx += 1\n            bi += 2\n        else:\n            bi += 1\n\n    return records\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run tests to verify they pass**\n\nRun: `uv run pytest libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_data.py -k \"normalize_mined_pairs\" -v`\nExpected: 9   "plan": "# Hypernetwork Training Dataset Pipeline\n\n> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.\n\n**Goal:** Build the dataset preparation pipeline that mines GitHub PRs into per-step training pairs with compressed diffs, outputting distillation-ready JSONL for S3 upload to train Rune's hypernetwork.\n\n**Architecture:** Three new components extend the existing mining pipeline: (1) `d2l_diff` module for RTK-style diff filtering, (2) `normalize_mined_pairs()` function producing one training record per review-to-revision cycle, (3) batch mining CLI processing a repos config. Output JSONL is compatible with existing `augment_trajectories()`, `split_by_task_id()`, and `save_jsonl()`. Mining runs on CPU; prepared JSONL uploads to S3 for L4 GPU training.\n\n**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12, httpx, json (stdlib), pytest\n\n---\n\n## Context\n\nRune's hypernetwork (Sakana Doc-to-LoRA perceiver) generates step-level LoRA adapters encoding procedural coding knowledge in weight space. The thesis: weight-space episodic memory outperforms context-window approaches for steering small models through complex multi-step coding projects.\n\nThis pipeline mines GitHub PRs from curated repositories, extracts per-step training pairs (review feedback -> code revision), compresses diffs to remove noise, and outputs distillation-ready JSONL. The data feeds Stage 1 (QLoRA fine-tuning with DeltaCoder warm-start on Qwen3.5-9B) which produces the adapter corpus for Stage 2 (hypernetwork training).\n\nEach training pair represents one step:\n- **activation_text** (hypernetwork input): PR description + current code state + review feedback\n- **teacher_text** (ground truth): activation_text + the correct revision diff\n\nKey design decisions from user interview:\n- Each adapter encodes ONE step, not an entire trajectory\n- \"Current Code\" = previous step's diff only (not cumulative)\n- Multi-file commits = one training pair (preserves cross-file coherence)\n- Merged PRs = positive examples; closed-unmerged = DPO negatives (tagged via metadata)\n- Diff compression filters lockfiles, generated code, binary files (RTK-style)\n- Minimum changes to existing code; reuse existing pipeline functions\n\n---\n\n## File Structure\n\n**Create:**\n- `libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_diff.py` -- diff compression (follows `d2l_*` naming convention)\n- `libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_diff.py` -- compressor tests\n- `instructions/mining_repos.json` -- Phase 1 repos config (10 repos)\n\n**Modify:**\n- `libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_data.py:20-29,653` -- add `normalize_mined_pairs()` + update `__all__`\n- `libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_data.py` -- add pair extraction tests\n- `scripts/mine_github.py:30-101,103-191` -- add batch mining mode\n\n**Existing code reused unchanged:**\n- `d2l_mining.py`: `mine_pr_diff_chains()`, `search_quality_prs()` -- mining core\n- `d2l_data.py`: `augment_trajectories()`, `split_by_task_id()`, `save_jsonl()`, `load_jsonl()` -- downstream pipeline\n- `github_client.py`: `GitHubClient` -- HTTP client with rate-limit retry\n\n---\n\n## Repo Selection Strategy\n\n**Phase 1 (Bootstrap): 10 repos, ~500 PRs, ~1500 step-level pairs**\n\n| Language | Repos | Rationale |\n|---|---|---|\n| Python (4) | huggingface/transformers, scikit-learn/scikit-learn, fastapi/fastapi, apache/airflow | Rich review culture, diverse domains (ML, algorithms, web, orchestration) |\n| TypeScript (2) | microsoft/TypeScript, vercel/next.js | Exceptional type-system reviews, high-velocity web |\n| Rust (2) | denoland/deno, rust-lang/rust-analyzer | Systems + IDE compiler, clean MIT |\n| Go (2) | pingcap/tidb, kubernetes/kubernetes | Bot-enforced issue linkage, richest review culture |\n\n50 quality-filtered PRs per repo via `search_quality_prs()`.\n\n**Phase 2 (Expand, after Stage 1 validation):** +15 repos adding C++ (llvm, duckdb, godot), C# (roslyn, runtime), Kotlin (okhttp), Dart (flutter), Scala (scala3), Solidity (OpenZeppelin), plus more Python/Rust/Go.\n\n**Phase 3 (Conditional):** Niche repos only if evaluation shows domain gaps.\n\n---\n\n### Task 1: Diff Compressor Module\n\n**Files:**\n- Create: `libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_diff.py`\n- Create: \"truncated\" in result_lines[-1]\n\n\ndef test_compress_diff_all_filtered_returns_empty() -> None:\n    \"\"\"When all sections are filtered, return empty string.\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_diff import compress_diff\n\n    content = (\n        \"--- package-lock.json ---\\n+lock\\n\"\n        \"--- yarn.lock ---\\n+yarn\\n\"\n        \"--- dist/out.js ---\\n+built\"\n    )\n    result = compress_diff(content)\n    assert result == \"\"\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 2: Run tests to verify they fail**\n\nRun: `uv run pytest libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_diff.py -v`\nExpected: FAIL (ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'model_training.d2l_diff')\n\n- [ ] **Step 3: Implement d2l_diff.py**\n\n```python\n# libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_diff.py\n\"\"\"RTK-style diff compression for training data preparation.\n\nFilters irrelevant files (lockfiles, generated code, binary assets,\nbuild artifacts) and truncates large diffs to minimize token overhead\nin hypernetwork training pairs.\n\nOperates on the concatenated diff format produced by mine_pr_diff_chains:\n    --- src/main.py ---\n    +real code\n    --- package-lock.json ---\n    +lockfile noise\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nfrom pathlib import PurePosixPath\n\n__all__ = [\"compress_diff\"]\n\n_SKIP_FILENAMES: frozenset[str] = frozenset(\n    {\n        \"package-lock.json\",\n        \"yarn.lock\",\n        \"pnpm-lock.yaml\",\n        \"poetry.lock\",\n        \"uv.lock\",\n        \"Pipfile.lock\",\n        \"Cargo.lock\",\n        \"go.sum\",\n        \"Gemfile.lock\",\n        \"composer.lock\",\n        \"flake.lock\",\n        \".DS_Store\",\n        \".gitattributes\",\n        \".editorconfig\",\n    }\n)\n\n_SKIP_EXTENSIONS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(\n    {\n        \".lock\",\n        \".png\",\n        \".jpg\",\n        \".jpeg\",\n        \".gif\",\n        \".svg\",\n        \".ico\",\n        \".woff\",\n        \".woff2\",\n        \".ttf\",\n        \".eot\",\n        \".mp3\",\n        \".mp4\",\n        \".zip\",\n        \".tar\",\n        \".gz\",\n        \".pdf\",\n        \".pyc\",\n        \".pyo\",\n        \".so\",\n        \".dylib\",\n        \".dll\",\n    }\n)\n\n_SKIP_SUFFIXES: tuple[str, ...] = (\n    \"_pb2.py\",\n    \"_pb2_grpc.py\",\n    \".pb.go\",\n    \".pb.h\",\n    \".pb.cc\",\n    \".generated.ts\",\n    \".generated.js\",\n    \".g.dart\",\n    \".min.js\",\n    \".min.css\",\n    \".map\",\n)\n\n_SKIP_PATH_SEGMENTS: tuple[str, ...] = (\n    \"node_modules/\",\n    \"__pycache__/\",\n    \".venv/\",\n    \"vendor/\",\n    \"dist/\",\n    \"build/\",\n    \".next/\",\n    \"coverage/\",\n    \".idea/\",\n    \".vscode/\",\n)\n\n_SECTION_RE = re.compile(r\"^(--- .+ ---)$\", re.MULTILINE)\n\n\ndef _should_skip(filename: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Check if a file should be filtered from training data.\"\"\"\n    path = PurePosixPath(filename)\n    if path.name in _SKIP_FILENAMES:\n        return True\n    if path.suffix in _SKIP_EXTENSIONS:\n        return True\n    if any(filename.endswith(s) for s in _SKIP_SUFFIXES):\n        return True\n    return any(seg in filename for seg in _SKIP_PATH_SEGMENTS)\n\n\ndef _truncate(text: str, max_lines: int) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Truncate text to max_lines, appending a marker if truncated.\"\"\"\n    lines = text.split(\"\\n\")\n    if len(lines) <= max_lines:\n        return text\n    kept = lines[:max_lines]\n    kept.append(f\"... ({len(lines) - max_lines} lines truncated)\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(kept)\n\n\ndef compress_diff(content: str, max_lines: int = 500) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Filter irrelevant files and truncate large diffs.\n\n    Parses the ``--- filename ---`` section format produced by\n    ``mine_pr_diff_chains`` and removes lockfiles, generated code,\n    binary files, and build artifacts.\n\n    Args:\n        content: Concatenated diff content with section headers.\n        max_lines: Maximum total output lines.\n\n    Returns:\n        Filtered and truncated diff string.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not content:\n        return content\n\n    parts = _SECTION_RE.split(content)\n\n    # No section headers found -- fall through to truncation only\n    if len(parts) == 1:\n        return _truncate(content, max_lines)\n\n    # parts = [preamble, header1, body1, header2, body2, ...]\n    # re.split with a capturing group interleaves captured groups\n    kept: list[str] = []\n    idx = 1  # skip preamble (parts[0])\n    while idx < len(parts):\n        header = parts[idx]\n        body = parts[idx + 1] if idx + 1 < len(parts) else \"\"\n        # Extract filename from \"--- filename ---\"\n        filename = header[4:-4]  # strip \"--- \" and \" ---\"\n        if not _should_skip(filename):\n            kept.append(header + body)\n        idx += 2\n\n    result = \"\".join(kept).strip()\n\n    if not result:\n        return \"\"\n\n    return _truncate(result, max_lines)\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run tests to verify they pass**\n\nRun: `uv run pytest libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_diff.py -v`\nExpected: 9 passed\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Run linting**\n\nRun: `uv run ruff check libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_diff.py libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_diff.py`\nExpected: clean\n\n- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_diff.py libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_diff.py\ngit commit -m \"feat: add d2l_diff module for RTK-style diff compression\"\n```\n\n---\n\n### Task 2: Per-Step Training Pair Extraction\n\n**Files:**\n- in d2l_data.py**\n\nAdd import at top of `d2l_data.py` (after existing imports, line ~16):\n\n```python\nfrom model_training.d2l_diff import compress_diff\n```\n\nAdd `\"normalize_mined_pairs\"` to `__all__` list (line ~20-29).\n\nAppend after `split_by_task_id` (after line 652):\n\n```python\ndef normalize_mined_pairs(\n    trajectory: dict[str, Any],\n    compress: bool = True,\n    max_diff_lines: int = 500,\n    language: str | None = None,\n) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Convert a mined PR trajectory into per-step training pairs.\n\n    Each review-to-revision cycle becomes one training record with\n    activation_text (task + current code + review feedback) and\n    teacher_text (activation + revision diff). Compatible with\n    ``augment_trajectories``, ``split_by_task_id``, and ``save_jsonl``.\n\n    The algorithm groups contiguous commits and reviews into blocks,\n    then pairs each reviews-block with the following commits-block.\n    Multiple commits in a block: the last commit is used (the state\n    the reviewer actually saw). Multiple reviews: concatenated.\n\n    Args:\n        trajectory: Raw mined trajectory from ``mine_pr_diff_chains``.\n        compress: Apply diff compression via ``compress_diff``.\n        max_diff_lines: Max lines per compressed diff.\n        language: Language tag for metadata (from repos config).\n\n    Returns:\n        List of training pair records with task_id, activation_text,\n        teacher_text, and metadata fields.\n    \"\"\"\n    task_id: str = trajectory.get(\"task_id\", \"\")\n    task_desc: str = trajectory.get(\"task_description\", \"\")\n    raw_steps: list[dict[str, Any]] = trajectory.get(\"steps\", [])\n    outcome: str = trajectory.get(\"outcome\", \"\")\n\n    if not raw_steps:\n        return []\n\n    # --- Group contiguous runs of commits / reviews ---\n    blocks: list[tuple[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]] = []\n    cur_type: str | None = None\n    cur_block: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    for step in raw_steps:\n        stype = step.get(\"type\", \"\")\n        if stype != cur_type:\n            if cur_block:\n                blocks.append((cur_type or \"\", cur_block))\n            cur_type = stype\n            cur_block = [step]\n        else:\n            cur_block.append(step)\n    if cur_block:\n        blocks.append((cur_type or \"\", cur_block))\n\n    def _diff(step: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n        raw = step.get(\"content\", \"\")\n        return compress_diff(raw, max_lines=max_diff_lines) if compress else raw\n\n    def _record(idx: int, activation: str, teacher: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        return {\n            \"task_id\": f\"{task_id}_step_{idx}\",\n            \"activation_text\": activation,\n            \"teacher_text\": teacher,\n            \"metadata\": {\n                \"outcome\": outcome,\n                \"step_index\": idx,\n                \"language\": language,\n                \"source_task_id\": task_id,\n            },\n        }\n\n    records: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    step_idx = 0\n    prev_diff = \"\"\n    bi = 0  # block index\n\n    # --- Step 0: initial commits block ---\n    if blocks[0][0] == \"commit\":\n        last_commit = blocks[0][1][-1]\n        diff = _diff(last_commit)\n        activation = f\"## Task\\n{task_desc}\"\n        teacher = f\"{activation}\\n\\n## Implementation\\n{diff}\"\n        records.append(_record(step_idx, activation, teacher))\n        prev_diff = diff\n        step_idx += 1\n        bi = 1\n\n    # --- Subsequent (reviews, commits) pairs ---\n    while bi < len(blocks) - 1:\n        if blocks[bi][0] == \"review\" and blocks[bi + 1][0] == \"commit\":\n            review_text = \"\\n\\n\".join(\n                r.get(\"content\", \"\") for r in blocks[bi][1]\n            )\n            last_commit = blocks[bi + 1][1][-1]\n            revision = _diff(last_commit)\n\n            activation = f\"## Task\\n{task_desc}\"\n            if prev_diff:\n                activation += f\"\\n\\n## Current Code\\n{prev_diff}\"\n            activation += f\"\\n\\n## Review Feedback\\n{review_text}\"\n            teacher = f\"{activation}\\n\\n## Revision\\n{revision}\"\n\n            records.append(_record(step_idx, activation, teacher))\n            prev_diff = revision\n            step_idx += 1\n            bi += 2\n        else:\n            bi += 1\n\n    return records\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 4: Run tests to verify they pass**\n\nRun: `uv run pytest libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_data.py -k \"normalize_mined_pairs\" -v`\nExpected: 9 passed\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Run full test suite + linting**\n\nRun: `uv run pytest libs/model-training/tests/ -v && uv run ruff check libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_data.py`\nExpected: All existing tests still pass + new tests pass + lint clean\n\n- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**\n\n```bash\ngit add libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_data.py \"repos.json\"\n    config_path.write_text(json.dumps(config))\n    return config_path\n\n\ndef _make_trajectory(repo: str, number: int) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Create a minimal mined New `_run_batch()` function\n5. `main()` dispatches based on which args are provided\n\n```python\n\"\"\"GitHub trajectory mining CLI.\n\nMines GitHub repositories into trajectory JSON files suitable for\nDoc-to-LoRA distillation. Supports single-repo mode (--repo) and\nbatch mode (--batch) for processing multiple repos from a config.\n\nUsage:\n    # Single repo\n    uv run python scripts/mine_github.py --repo owner/repo -o out.json\n    uv run python scripts/mine_github.py --repo owner/repo --quality -o prs.json\n\n    # Batch mode (produces per-repo JSONL with training pairs)\n    uv run python scripts/mine_github.py --batch instructions/mining_repos.json --output-dir data/pairs/\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format=\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(name)s: %(message)s\",\n)\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\ndef parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:\n    \"\"\"Parse command-line arguments.\"\"\"\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Mine GitHub repositories into trajectory files.\",\n        formatter_class=argparse.ArgumentDefaultsHelpFormatter,\n    )\n    # --- Single-repo mode ---\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--repo\",\n        metavar=\"OWNER/REPO\",\n        help='GitHub repository in \"owner/repo\" format (single-repo mode).',\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"-o\",\n        \"--output\",\n        type=Path,\n        metavar=\"FILE\",\n        help=\"Output JSON file path (single-repo mode).\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--normalize\",\n        action=\"store_true\",\n        help=\"Normalize trajectories for distillation compatibility (single-repo mode).\",\n    )\n    # --- Batch mode ---\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--batch\",\n        type=Path,\n        metavar=\"CONFIG\",\n        help=\"Path to JSON repos config for batch mining.\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--output-dir\",\n        type=Path,\n        metavar=\"DIR\",\n        help=\"Output directory for batch mode (one JSONL per repo).\",\n    )\n    # --- Shared options ---\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--mode\",\n        choices=[\"prs\", \"issues\", \"both\"],\n        default=\"both\",\n        help=\"Mining mode: pull requests, issues, or both.\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--max\",\n        type=int,\n        default=100,\n        dest=\"max_items\",\n        metavar=\"N\",\n        help=\"Maximum PRs/issues to process.\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--token\",\n        default=None,\n        metavar=\"TOKEN\",\n        help=\"GitHub personal access token (falls back to GITHUB_TOKEN env var).\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--quality\",\n        action=\"store_true\",\n        help=\"Pre-filter PRs via GitHub Search API for quality.\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--min-reviews\",\n        type=int,\n        default=1,\n        metavar=\"N\",\n        help=\"Minimum review comments for quality filter.\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--min-commits\",\n        type=int,\n        default=2,\n        metavar=\"N\",\n        help=\"Minimum commits for quality filter.\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--exclude-labels\",\n        default=None,\n        metavar=\"L1,L2,...\",\n        help=\"Comma-separated labels to exclude.\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--max-diff-lines\",\n        type=int,\n        default=500,\n        metavar=\"N\",\n        help=\"Maximum lines per compressed diff in batch mode.\",\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    # Validate argument combinations\n    if args.batch:\n        if not args.output_dir:\n            parser.error(\"--output-dir is required with --batch\")\n    elif args.repo:\n        if not args.output:\n            parser.error(\"-o/--output is required with --repo\")\n    else:\n        parser.error(\"Either --repo or --batch is required\")\n\n    return args\n\n\ndef _run_single(args: argparse.Namespace, token: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Run single-repo mining (original behavior).\"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_data import normalize_mined_trajectory\n    from model_training.d2l_mining import (\n        mine_issue_commit_chains,\n        mine_pr_diff_chains,\n        search_quality_prs,\n    )\n\n    exclude_labels: list[str] | None = None\n    if args.exclude_labels:\n        exclude_labels = [lbl.strip() for lbl in args.exclude_labels.split(\",\")]\n\n    trajectories: list[dict] = []\n\n    if args.mode in (\"prs\", \"both\"):\n        pr_numbers: list[int] | None = None\n        if args.quality:\n            logger.info(\n                \"Searching for quality PRs in %s (min_reviews=%d, min_commits=%d)...\",\n                args.repo,\n                args.min_reviews,\n                args.min_commits,\n            )\n            pr_numbers = search_quality_prs(\n                args.repo,\n                max_results=args.max_items,\n                github_token=token,\n                min_review_comments=args.min_reviews,\n                min_commits=args.min_commits,\n                exclude_labels=exclude_labels,\n            )\n            logger.info(\"Quality filter selected %d PR(s).\", len(pr_numbers))\n\n        logger.info(\n            \"Mining PR diff chains from %s (max=%d)...\", args.repo, args.max_items\n        )\n        pr_trajectories = mine_pr_diff_chains(\n            args.repo,\n            max_prs=args.max_items,\n            github_token=token,\n            pr_numbers=pr_numbers,\n        )\n        logger.info(\"Mined %d PR diff chain(s).\", len(pr_trajectories))\n        trajectories.extend(pr_trajectories)\n\n    if args.mode in (\"issues\", \"both\"):\n        logger.info(\n            \"Mining issue-commit chains from %s (max=%d)...\",\n            args.repo,\n            args.max_items,\n        )\n        issue_trajectories = mine_issue_commit_chains(\n            args.repo,\n            max_issues=args.max_items,\n            github_token=token,\n        )\n        logger.info(\"Mined %d issue-commit chain(s).\", len(issue_trajectories))\n        trajectories.extend(issue_trajectories)\n\n    logger.info(\"Total trajectories collected: %d.\", len(trajectories))\n\n    if args.normalize:\n        logger.info(\"Normalizing %d trajectory/trajectories...\", len(trajectories))\n        trajectories = [normalize_mined_trajectory(t) for t in trajectories]\n        logger.info(\"Normalization complete.\")\n\n    output: Path = args.output\n    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    with output.open(\"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as fh:\n        json.dump(trajectories, fh, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n    logger.info(\"Wrote %d trajectory/trajectories to %s.\", len(trajectories), output)\n\n\ndef _run_batch(\n    config_path: Path,\n    output_dir: Path,\n    token: str,\n    compress: bool = True,\n    max_diff_lines: int = 500,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Run batch mining from a repos config file.\n\n    Processes each repo in the config, extracts training pairs via\n    normalize_mined_pairs, and saves per-repo JSONL files.\n    \"\"\"\n    from model_training.d2l_data import normalize_mined_pairs, save_jsonl\n    from model_training.d2l_mining import mine_pr_diff_chains, search_quality_prs\n\n    config = json.loads(config_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    defaults = config.get(\"defaults\", {})\n    repos = config.get(\"repos\", [])\n\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    total_pairs = 0\n\n    for repo_cfg in repos:\n        repo = repo_cfg[\"repo\"]\n        language = repo_cfg.get(\"language\")\n        max_prs = repo_cfg.get(\"max_prs\", defaults.get(\"max_prs\", 50))\n        quality = repo_cfg.get(\"quality\", defaults.get(\"quality\", True))\n        min_reviews = repo_cfg.get(\n            \"min_review_comments\", defaults.get(\"min_review_comments\", 1)\n        )\n        min_commits = repo_cfg.get(\n            \"min_commits\", defaults.get(\"min_commits\", 2)\n        )\n\n        logger.info(\"Mining %s (max=%d, quality=%s)...\", repo, max_prs, quality)\n\n        pr_numbers: list[int] | None = None\n        if quality:\n            pr_numbers = search_quality_prs(\n                repo,\n                max_results=max_prs,\n                github_token=token,\n                min_review_comments=min_reviews,\n                min_commits=min_commits,\n            )\n            logger.info(\"Quality filter: %d PRs for %s\", len(pr_numbers), repo)\n\n        trajectories = mine_pr_diff_chains(\n            repo, max_prs=max_prs, github_token=token, pr_numbers=pr_numbers\n        )\n        logger.info(\"Mined %d trajectories from %s\", len(trajectories), repo)\n\n        pairs: list[dict] = []\n        for traj in trajectories:\n            pairs.extend(\n                normalize_mined_pairs(\n                    traj,\n                    compress=compress,\n                    max_diff_lines=max_diff_lines,\n                    language=language,\n                )\n            )\n\n        filename = repo.replace(\"/\", \"_\") + \".jsonl\"\n        save_jsonl(pairs, output_dir / filename)\n        logger.info(\"Saved %d pairs to %s\", len(pairs), filename)\n        total_pairs += len(pairs)\n\n    logger.info(\n        \"Batch complete: %d total pairs across %d repos\", total_pairs, len(repos)\n    )\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Entry point for the mine_github CLI.\"\"\"\n    args = parse_args()\n\n    token: str | None = args.token or os.environ.get(\"GITHUB_TOKEN\")\n    if not token:\n        logger.error(\n            \"No GitHub token. Use --token or set the GITHUB_TOKEN env var.\"\n        )\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    if args.batch:\n        _run_batch(\n            config_path=args.batch,\n            output_dir=args.output_dir,\n            token=token,\n            max_diff_lines=args.max_diff_lines,\n        )\n    else:\n        _run_single(args, token)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n```\n\n- [ ] **Step 5: Run tests to verify they pass**\n\nRun: `uv run pytest libs/model-training/tests/test_mine_batch.py -v`\nExpected: 2 passed (the batch function test + config validation ## Approved Plan:
# Hypernetwork Training Dataset Pipeline

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build the dataset preparation pipeline that mines GitHub PRs into per-step training pairs with compressed diffs, outputting distillation-ready JSONL for S3 upload to train Rune's hypernetwork.

**Architecture:** Three new components extend the existing mining pipeline: (1) `d2l_diff` module for RTK-style diff filtering, (2) `normalize_mined_pairs()` function producing one training record per review-to-revision cycle, (3) batch mining CLI processing a repos config. Output JSONL is compatible with existing `augment_trajectories()`, `split_by_task_id()`, and `save_jsonl()`. Mining runs on CPU; prepared JSONL uploads to S3 for L4 GPU training.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12, httpx, json (stdlib), pytest

---

## Context

Rune's hypernetwork (Sakana Doc-to-LoRA perceiver) generates step-level LoRA adapters encoding procedural coding knowledge in weight space. The thesis: weight-space episodic memory outperforms context-window approaches for steering small models through complex multi-step coding projects.

This pipeline mines GitHub PRs from curated repositories, extracts per-step training pairs (review feedback -> code revision), compresses diffs to remove noise, and outputs distillation-ready JSONL. The data feeds Stage 1 (QLoRA fine-tuning with DeltaCoder warm-start on Qwen3.5-9B) which produces the adapter corpus for Stage 2 (hypernetwork training).

Each training pair represents one step:
- **activation_text** (hypernetwork input): PR description + current code state + review feedback
- **teacher_text** (ground truth): activation_text + the correct revision diff

Key design decisions from user interview:
- Each adapter encodes ONE step, not an entire trajectory
- "Current Code" = previous step's diff only (not cumulative)
- Multi-file commits = one training pair (preserves cross-file coherence)
- Merged PRs = positive examples; closed-unmerged = DPO negatives (tagged via metadata)
- Diff compression filters lockfiles, generated code, binary files (RTK-style)
- Minimum changes to existing code; reuse existing pipeline functions

---

**Create:**
- `libs/model-training/src/model_training/d2l_diff.py` -- diff compression (follows `d2l_*` naming convention)
- `libs/model-training/tests/test_d2l_diff.py` -- compressor tests
- `instructions/mining_repos.json` -- Phase 1 repos config (10 repos)

**Modify:**
